Encoding Jump to heading ¶

Project Description Jump to heading ¶

CELT: Corpus of Electronic Texts

Sampling Declaration Jump to heading ¶

The present text represents pages 1–260 of the volume. All editorial introduction, notes and indexes have been omitted.

Editorial Declaration Jump to heading ¶

Correction Jump to heading ¶

Text has been checked and proofread twice. Missing text supplied by the editor is tagged sup.

Normalization Jump to heading ¶

The electronic text represents the edited text. Expansions to the text are marked ex.

Hyphenation Jump to heading ¶

Soft hyphens are silently removed.

Segmentation Jump to heading ¶

div0=the whole document; div1=the section; stanzas are marked lg.

Interpretation Jump to heading ¶

Names of persons (given names), and places are not tagged. Terms for cultural and social roles are not tagged.

Canonical References Jump to heading ¶

This text uses the DIV1 element to represent the section. ### Profile Description

Created: By Irish scribes in monastic scriptoria, based on older materials. Date range: 1150–1190.#### Use of language

Language: [GA] The text is in Middle Irish. Jump to heading ¶
Language: [LA] Some words and phrases are in Latin. Jump to heading ¶

Corpus of Electronic Texts Edition: G800011A Jump to heading ¶

Book of Leinster, formerly Lebar na Núachongbála: Author: [unknown] Jump to heading ¶

p.1

Lebor Gabála Jump to heading ¶

{MS folio 1a 1}IN PRINCIPIO fecit Deus celum & terram .i. Doringne {MS folio 1a 5}Dia nem & talmain ar tús & ni fil tossach na forcend fairseom féin. Doringne chétus in maiss nemchruthaig & soillsi angel isin cetna domnuch. Doringne firmimint isin luan.

5] {MS folio 1a 10} Doringni talmain & muire sin máirt. Doringne grein & ésca & renna nime sin cétain. Doringne enlaithe ind aeóir & tonnaitecha in mara {MS folio 1a 15}sin dardain. 7480] ó Cherball mac Muricain do thig a athar & ro chuiredar ra haiss arís d'indsaigid Cerbaill. i cinta ind imdergtha tuc Cerball {MS folio 52 b5}di. Cerball i n-othrus in tansain i nNás. Is i do iarfaigid scela in chatha Maigi Ailbi & amal ra marbad Cormac. Ra boísium iarum

7485] oca innisin dissi mar ra gatad a chend de Chormac & se fein na fiadnaisi. Is and atrubairt Gormlaith.

for dreich na derna maith

7490] don epscop don ríg don flaith. &c.
